```coq
Lemma multi_trans : forall t1 t2 t3, t1 -->* t2 -> t2 -->* t3 -> t1 -->* t3.
Proof.
  intros t1 t2 t3 H12 H23. revert t3 H23.
  induction H12 as [x | x y z Hxy Hyz IHyz]; intros t3 H23.
  - assumption.
  - apply multi_step with (y := y); [assumption | apply IHyz; assumption].
Qed.

Lemma multi_app_l : forall t1 t1' t2,
  t1 -->* t1' -> lc_tm_at 0 0 t2 -> tm_app t1 t2 -->* tm_app t1' t2.
Proof.
  intros t1 t1' t2 Hlc. induction H as [x | x y z Hxy Hyz IHyz].
  ...
```
Hmm, order: H : t1 -->* t1' and Hlc : lc t2. Let me do `intros t1 t1' t2 Hmult Hlc. induction Hmult ...`. In the step case: x --> y, y -->* z, IH: app y t2 -->* app z t2. Then app x t2 --> app y t2 by ST_App1 x y t2 Hxy Hlc, then multi_step ✓.

```coq
Lemma multi_app_l : forall t1 t2 t1',
  t1 -->* t1' -> lc_tm_at 0 0 t2 -> tm_app t1 t2 -->* tm_app t1' t2.
Proof.
  intros t1 t2 t1' H Hlc. induction H as [x | x y z Hxy Hyz IHyz].
  - apply multi_refl.
  - apply multi_step with (y := tm_app y t2).
    + apply ST_App1; assumption.
    + apply IHyz.
Qed.

Lemma multi_app_r : forall v1 t2 t2',
  value v1 -> t2 -->* t2' -> tm_app v1 t2 -->* tm_app v1 t2'.
Proof.
  intros v1 t2 t2' Hv H. induction H as [x | x y z Hxy Hyz IHyz].
  - apply multi_refl.
  - apply multi_step with (y := tm_app v1 y).
    + apply ST_App2; assumption.
    + apply IHyz.
Qed.

Lemma multi_tapp : forall t t' U,
  t -->* t' -> lc_ty_at 0 U -> tm_tapp t U -->* tm_tapp t' U.
Proof.
  intros t t' U H Hlc. induction H as [x | x y z Hxy Hyz IHyz].
  - apply multi_refl.
  - apply multi_step with (y := tm_tapp y U).
    + apply ST_TApp; assumption.
    + apply IHyz.
Qed.

Lemma multi_app_beta : forall T body v,
  lc_tm_at 0 0 (tm_abs T body) -> value v ->
  tm_app (tm_abs T body) v -->* open_tm body v.
Proof. intros. apply multi_step with (y := open_tm body v). apply ST_AppAbs; assumption. apply multi_refl. Qed.

Lemma multi_tapp_tabs : forall body U,
  lc_tm_at 0 0 (tm_tabs body) -> lc_ty_at 0 U ->
  tm_tapp (tm_tabs body) U -->* open_tm_ty body U.
Proof. intros. apply multi_step with (y := open_tm_ty body U). apply ST_TAppTabs; assumption. apply multi_refl. Qed.

Lemma value_lc : forall v, value v -> lc_tm_at 0 0 v.
Proof. intros v H. inversion H; subst; assumption. Qed.
```

Careful with value's constructors: 
```
| v_abs : forall T t, locally_closed_tm (tm_abs T t) -> value (tm_abs T t)
| v_tabs : forall t, locally_closed_tm (tm_tabs t) -> value (tm_tabs t)
```
inversion H gives exactly lc ✓.

Now the relation. Let me write the definitions:

```coq
Fixpoint Vty (env : list Rel) (xi : atom -> Rel) (T : ty) : Rel :=
  match T with
  | Ty_BVar i => fun v1 v2 => value v1 /\ value v2 /\ nth i env bot_rel v1 v2
  | Ty_FVar X => fun v1 v2 => value v1 /\ value v2 /\ xi X v1 v2
  | Ty_Arrow A B => fun v1 v2 =>
      value v1 /\ value v2 /\
      (forall w1 w2, Vty env xi A w1 w2 ->
         exists u1 u2, tm_app v1 w1 -->* u1 /\ tm_app v2 w2 -->* u2 /\
                       Vty env xi B u1 u2)
  | Ty_All T1 => fun v1 v2 =>
      value v1 /\ value v2 /\
      (forall (rho : Rel) (U1 U2 : ty),
         lc_ty_at 0 U1 -> lc_ty_at 0 U2 ->
         exists u1 u2, tm_tapp v1 U1 -->* u1 /\ tm_tapp v2 U2 -->* u2 /\
                       Vty (rho :: env) xi T1 u1 u2)
  end.

Definition Rty (env : list Rel) (xi : atom -> Rel) (T : ty) : Rel :=
  fun t1 t2 => lc_tm_at 0 0 t1 /\ lc_tm_at 0 0 t2 /\
    exists v1 v2, t1 -->* v1 /\ t2 -->* v2 /\ Vty env xi T v1 v2.
```

Wait, I need to double-check the Vty Arrow clause: it quantifies over values w1 w2 with Vty A, and produces the existential for the application. ✓.

Note: V_arrow does not require the argument's lc separately—Vty A implies value → lc ✓.

Now the relation lemmas:

```
  - destruct H as [Hv1 [Hv2 _]]. auto.
  - destruct H as [Hv1 [Hv2 _]]. auto.
  - destruct H as [Hv1 [Hv2 _]]. auto.
  - destruct H as [Hv1 [Hv2 _]]. auto.
```

Now, the fundamental lemma statement. Let me write it:

```
Lemma fundamental : forall Delta Gamma t T,
  has_type Delta Gamma t T ->
  forall env xi theta1 theta2 sigma1 sigma2,
    (forall X, lc_ty_at 0 (theta1 X)) ->
    (forall X, lc_ty_at 0 (theta2 X)) ->
    (forall x, lc_tm_at 0 0 (sigma1 x)) ->
    (forall x, lc_tm_at 0 0 (sigma2 x)) ->
    (forall x Ti, lookup_context x Gamma = Some Ti ->
       Rty env xi Ti (sigma1 x) (sigma2 x)) ->
    Rty env xi T (tm_subst_env sigma1 (tm_ty_subst_env theta1 t))
                 (tm_subst_env sigma2 (tm_ty_subst_env theta2 t)).
```

Proof by induction on the has_type derivation. Let me write each case.

Since Rty is a plain definition, `unfold Rty in H` and `destruct` works, but helper lemmas make proofs cleaner.

**T_App**: t = tm_app t1 t2, T = T2.

Note on `multi` induction naming: multi constructors:
```
| multi_refl : forall x, multi x x
```

Induction pattern: `induction H as [x | x y z Hxy Hyz IH]`.

Let me write:

```coq
Lemma multi_trans : forall t1 t2 t3, t1 -->* t2 -> t2 -->* t3 -> t1 -->* t3.
Proof.
  intros t1 t2 t3 H12. revert t3.
  induction H12 as [x | x y z Hxy Hyz IH]; intros t3 H23.
  - assumption.
  - apply multi_step with (y := y); [assumption | apply IH; assumption].
Qed.
```

All the syntactic development compiles now. Time to define the logical relation and prove its lemmas, then the fundamental lemma.

Let me write the relation definition and the basic lemmas:

```coq
(* ---------- The logical relation ---------- *)

Fixpoint Vty (env : list Rel) (xi : atom -> Rel) (T : ty) : Rel :=
  match T with
  | Ty_BVar i => fun v1 v2 => value v1 /\ value v2 /\ nth i env bot_rel v1 v2
  | Ty_FVar X => fun v1 v2 => value v1 /\ value v2 /\ xi X v1 v2
  | Ty_Arrow A B => fun v1 v2 =>
      value v1 /\ value v2 /\
      (forall w1 w2, Vty env xi A w1 w2 ->
         exists u1 u2, tm_app v1 w1 -->* u1 /\ tm_app v2 w2 -->* u2 /\
                       Vty env xi B u1 u2)
  | Ty_All T1 => fun v1 v2 =>
      value v1 /\ value v2 /\
      (forall (rho : Rel) (U1 U2 : ty),
         lc_ty_at 0 U1 -> lc_ty_at 0 U2 ->
         exists u1 u2, tm_tapp v1 U1 -->* u1 /\ tm_tapp v2 U2 -->* u2 /\
                       Vty (rho :: env) xi T1 u1 u2)
  end.

Definition Rty (env : list Rel) (xi : atom -> Rel) (T : ty) : Rel :=
  fun t1 t2 =>
    lc_tm_at 0 0 t1 /\ lc_tm_at 0 0 t2 /\
    exists v1 v2, t1 -->* v1 /\ t2 -->* v2 /\ Vty env xi T v1 v2.
```

Lemmas:

```coq
Lemma Vty_value : forall env xi T v1 v2, Vty env xi T v1 v2 -> value v1 /\ value v2.
Proof.
  intros env xi T. induction T as [n | X | A IHA B IHB | T1 IH1]; intros v1 v2 H; simpl in H.
  - destruct H as [H1 [H2 H3]]. auto.
  - destruct H as [H1 [H2 H3]]. auto.
  - destruct H as [H1 [H2 H3]]. auto.
  - destruct H as [H1 [H2 H3]]. auto.
Qed.
```
Since `simpl in H` will beta-reduce the match on the constructor, each H is `value v1 /\ value v2 /\ _` ✓. `destruct ... as [H1 [H2 H3]]; auto` gives both ✓.

```coq
Lemma Vty_lc : forall env xi T v1 v2, Vty env xi T v1 v2 -> lc_tm_at 0 0 v1 /\ lc_tm_at 0 0 v2.
Proof.
  intros. apply Vty_value in H. destruct H. split; apply value_lc; assumption.
Qed.
```

Env agreement:
```coq
Lemma Vty_env_agree : forall d T env1 env2 xi,
  lc_ty_at d T ->
  (forall i, i < d -> nth i env1 bot_rel = nth i env2 bot_rel) ->
  forall v1 v2, Vty env1 xi T v1 v2 <-> Vty env2 xi T v1 v2.
Proof.
  intros d T. revert d.
  induction T as [n | X | A IHA B IHB | T1 IH1]; intros d env1 env2 xi Hlc Hagree v1 v2; simpl.
  - inversion Hlc as [d0 i Hi | d0 X0 | d0 A0 B0 HA HB | d0 T0 H0]; subst.
    split; intros [H1 [H2 H3]]; split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
    + rewrite <- (Hagree n Hi). assumption.
    + rewrite (Hagree n Hi). assumption.
  - split; intros H; exact H.
  - inversion Hlc as [d0 i Hi | d0 X0 | d0 A0 B0 HA HB | d0 T0 H0]; subst.
    split; intros [H1 [H2 H3]]; split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
    + intros w1 w2 Hw. apply (proj2 (IHA d env1 env2 xi HA Hagree w1 w2)) in Hw.
      destruct (H3 w1 w2 Hw) as [u1 [u2 [R1 [R2 HV]]]].
      exists u1, u2. split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
      exact (proj1 (IHB d env1 env2 xi HB Hagree u1 u2) HV).
    + intros w1 w2 Hw. apply (proj1 (IHA d env1 env2 xi HA Hagree w1 w2)) in Hw.
      destruct (H3 w1 w2 Hw) as [u1 [u2 [R1 [R2 HV]]]].
      exists u1, u2. split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
      exact (proj2 (IHB d env1 env2 xi HB Hagree u1 u2) HV).
  - inversion Hlc as [d0 i Hi | d0 X0 | d0 A0 B0 HA HB | d0 T0 H0]; subst.
    split; intros [H1 [H2 H3]]; split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
    + intros rho U1 U2 HU1 HU2.
      destruct (H3 rho U1 U2 HU1 HU2) as [u1 [u2 [R1 [R2 HV]]]].
      exists u1, u2. split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
      apply (proj1 (IH1 (S d) (rho :: env1) (rho :: env2) xi H0 Hagree' u1 u2)). exact HV.
    + ...
```
I need the Hagree' for the extended envs. Let me define it inline:
```
      assert (Hagree' : forall i, i < S d -> nth i (rho :: env1) bot_rel = nth i (rho :: env2) bot_rel).
      { intros i Hi. destruct i as [| i'].
        - reflexivity.
        - simpl. apply Hagree. lia. }
```
Careful: `nth (S i') (rho :: env1) bot_rel` simplifies to `nth i' env1 bot_rel` ✓.

In the forward direction (env1 → env2), the IH gives Vty env1 A → Vty env2 A, i.e., proj1; and Vty env1 B → Vty env2 B, proj1 ✓. In the backward direction (goal env1), we need Vty env2 A → Vty env1 A = proj2; and Vty env2 B → Vty env1 B = proj2 ✓.

```coq
Lemma Vty_xi_agree : forall T env xi1 xi2,
  (forall X, In X (ty_fv T) -> xi1 X = xi2 X) ->
  forall v1 v2, Vty env xi1 T v1 v2 <-> Vty env xi2 T v1 v2.

  intros T. induction T as [n | X | A IHA B IHB | T1 IH1]; intros env xi1 xi2 Hagree v1 v2; simpl.
  - split; intros H; exact H.
  - split; intros [H1 [H2 H3]]; split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
    + rewrite <- (Hagree X). assumption. simpl; auto.
    + rewrite (Hagree X). assumption. simpl; auto.
  - split; intros [H1 [H2 H3]]; split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
    + intros w1 w2 Hw. apply (proj2 (IHA env xi1 xi2 ...)) in Hw. ...
```

```coq
Lemma Vty_open_ty : forall env xi k T U,
  k <= length env ->
  lc_ty_at 0 U ->
  lc_ty_at (k+1) T ->
  forall v1 v2,
    Vty env xi (open_ty_rec k U T) v1 v2 <->
    Vty (insert_at k (Vty env xi U) env) xi T v1 v2.
Proof.
  intros env xi k T. revert env xi k.
  induction T as [n | X | A IHA B IHB | T1 IH1];
    intros env xi k Hlen HU Hlc v1 v2; simpl.
```
Cases:
- BVar n: simpl: open_ty_rec k U (Ty_BVar n) = if Nat.eqb k n then U else Ty_BVar n. destruct (Nat.eqb k n) eqn:E.

    (* LHS: Vty env xi U v1 v2; RHS: Vty (insert_at k (Vty env xi U) env) xi (Ty_BVar k) = value v1 ∧ value v2 ∧ nth k (insert_at k R env) bot v1 v2 *)

    * destruct (Vty_value env xi U v1 v2 H) as [Hv1 Hv2].
      split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
      rewrite nth_insert_at_eq; [assumption | assumption].
    * destruct H as [Hv1 [Hv2 H3]]. rewrite nth_insert_at_eq in H3; [assumption | assumption].
  + (* k ≠ n; Hlc : lc_ty_at (k+1) (BVar n) → n < k+1; with n ≠ k → n < k *)
    inversion Hlc as [d0 i Hi | d0 X0 | d0 A0 B0 HA HB | d0 T0 H0]; subst.
    (* Hi : n < k+1; need n < k *)
    assert (Hnk : n < k) by lia.
    (* LHS: Vty env xi (BVar n); RHS: Vty (insert_at k R env) xi (BVar n) *)

    * rewrite <- (nth_insert_at_lt env k (Vty env xi U) n Hlen Hnk). assumption.
    * rewrite (nth_insert_at_lt env k (Vty env xi U) n Hlen Hnk). assumption.

- Arrow A B: Hlc : lc_ty_at (k+1) (A→B); inversion gives HA, HB.
  LHS = value∧value∧(forall w1 w2, Vty env xi (open_ty_rec k U A) w1 w2 -> exists ... Vty env xi (open_ty_rec k U B) u1 u2)
  RHS = value∧value∧(forall w1 w2, Vty (ins k R env) xi A w1 w2 -> ... Vty (ins k R env) xi B u1 u2)
  Use IHA / IHB iff at k with Hlen, HU, HA/HB.
- All T1: Hlc : lc_ty_at (k+1) (Ty_All T1) → H0 : lc_ty_at (k+2) T1.
  LHS = value∧value∧ forall rho U1 U2, lc -> exists u1 u2, tapp v1 U1 -->* u1 ∧ ... ∧ Vty (rho::env) xi (open_ty_rec (k+1) U T1) u1 u2.
  RHS = value∧value∧ forall rho U1 U2, lc -> exists ... ∧ Vty (rho :: insert_at k R env) xi T1 u1 u2.
  Note open_ty_rec k U (Ty_All T1) = Ty_All (open_ty_rec (k+1) U T1) ✓.
  For each rho: apply IH1 with env := rho::env, k := k+1, Hlen' : k+1 ≤ length (rho::env) (from Hlen: k ≤ length env → S k ≤ S (length env) = length (rho::env) ✓), HU, H0.
  The IH gives Vty (rho::env) xi (open_ty_rec (k+1) U T1) ↔ Vty (insert_at (k+1) (Vty (rho::env) xi U) (rho::env)) xi T1.
  Need: Vty (rho::env) xi U = Vty env xi U (by Vty_env_agree with d=0 and U lc_ty_at 0: envs rho::env and env agree on the first 0 entries trivially ✓).
  Need: insert_at (k+1) R (rho::env) = rho :: insert_at k R env — true by definition of insert_at when the env is non-empty and k+1 = S k ✓ definitional? insert_at (S k) R (rho :: env) = rho :: insert_at k R env ✓ by simpl.
  So the IH gives the needed iff.

Let me write Vty_open_ty in full:

```coq
Lemma Vty_open_ty : forall env xi k T U,
  k <= length env ->
  lc_ty_at 0 U ->
  lc_ty_at (k+1) T ->
  forall v1 v2,
    Vty env xi (open_ty_rec k U T) v1 v2 <->
    Vty (insert_at k (Vty env xi U) env) xi T v1 v2.
Proof.
  intros env xi k T. revert env xi k.
  induction T as [n | X | A IHA B IHB | T1 IH1];
    intros env xi k Hlen HU Hlc v1 v2; simpl.
  - destruct (Nat.eqb k n) eqn:E.
    + apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. subst n.
      split; intro H.
      * destruct (Vty_value env xi U v1 v2 H) as [Hv1 Hv2].
        split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
        rewrite nth_insert_at_eq; [assumption | assumption].
      * destruct H as [Hv1 [Hv2 H3]].
        rewrite nth_insert_at_eq in H3; [assumption | assumption].
    + inversion Hlc as [d0 i Hi | d0 X0 | d0 A0 B0 HA HB | d0 T0 H0]; subst.
      assert (Hnk : n < k) by lia.
      split; intros [H1 [H2 H3]]; split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
      * rewrite <- (nth_insert_at_lt env k (Vty env xi U) n Hlen Hnk). assumption.
      * rewrite (nth_insert_at_lt env k (Vty env xi U) n Hlen Hnk). assumption.
  - split; intros H; exact H.
  - inversion Hlc as [d0 i Hi | d0 X0 | d0 A0 B0 HA HB | d0 T0 H0]; subst.
    split; intros [H1 [H2 H3]]; split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
    + intros w1 w2 Hw.
      apply (proj2 (IHA env xi k HA Hlen HU w1 w2)) in Hw.
      destruct (H3 w1 w2 Hw) as [u1 [u2 [R1 [R2 HV]]]].
      exists u1, u2. split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
      exact (proj1 (IHB env xi k HB Hlen HU u1 u2) HV).
    + intros w1 w2 Hw.
      apply (proj1 (IHA env xi k HA Hlen HU w1 w2)) in Hw.
      destruct (H3 w1 w2 Hw) as [u1 [u2 [R1 [R2 HV]]]].
      exists u1, u2. split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
      exact (proj2 (IHB env xi k HB Hlen HU u1 u2) HV).
  - inversion Hlc as [d0 i Hi | d0 X0 | d0 A0 B0 HA HB | d0 T0 H0]; subst.
    split; intros [H1 [H2 H3]]; split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
    + intros rho U1 U2 HU1 HU2.
      destruct (H3 rho U1 U2 HU1 HU2) as [u1 [u2 [R1 [R2 HV]]]].
      exists u1, u2. split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
      assert (Henv : Vty (rho :: env) xi U = Vty env xi U).
      { apply (Vty_env_agree 0 U (rho :: env) env xi HU). intros i Hi. lia. }
      rewrite Henv in HV.
      assert (Hins : insert_at (k+1) (Vty env xi U) (rho :: env) = rho :: insert_at k (Vty env xi U) env).
      { simpl. reflexivity. }
      rewrite <- Hins.
      apply (proj1 (IH1 (rho :: env) xi (k+1) H0 ?len HU u1 u2)). exact HV.
    + ...
```
Hmm, careful with the argument order of IH1: IH1 : forall env xi k, k <= length env -> lc_ty_at 0 U -> lc_ty_at (k+1) T1 -> forall v1 v2, Vty env xi (open_ty_rec k U T1) v1 v2 ↔ Vty (insert_at k (Vty env xi U) env) xi T1 v1 v2. ```

```coq
Lemma Vty_open_fvar : forall env xi k T X rho,
  k <= length env ->
  ~ In X (ty_fv T) ->
  lc_ty_at (k+1) T ->
  forall v1 v2,

    Vty (insert_at k rho env) xi T v1 v2.
Proof.
  intros env xi k T. revert env xi k.
  induction T as [n | Y | A IHA B IHB | T1 IH1];
    intros env xi k X rho Hlen Hfresh Hlc v1 v2; simpl.
  - destruct (Nat.eqb k n) eqn:E.
    + apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. subst n.
      (* LHS: Vty env xi' (FVar X) = value ∧ value ∧ xi' X = rho *)

      split; intros H.

        split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
        rewrite nth_insert_at_eq in H3; [| assumption].
        rewrite Hupd. assumption.
```

```
Lemma insert_at_agree : forall (r r' : Rel) env k,
  (forall a b, r a b <-> r' a b) ->
  k <= length env ->
  forall i, i < k+1 -> forall a b,

  destruct (Nat.eq_lt_or_gt ...) as [E | Hlt]? 
  - if i = k: nth k (insert k r env) = r and similarly r' → Hiff.
  - if i < k: both nth i env.
```

```coq
Lemma Vty_open_ty : forall env xi k T U R,
  k <= length env ->
  lc_ty_at 0 U ->
  lc_ty_at (S k) T ->
  (forall a b, R a b <-> Vty env xi U a b) ->
  forall v1 v2,
    Vty env xi (open_ty_rec k U T) v1 v2 <->
    Vty (insert_at k R env) xi T v1 v2.
Proof.
  intros env xi k T U R. revert env xi k R.
  induction T as [n | X | A IHA B IHB | T1 IH1];
    intros env xi k R Hlen HU Hlc Hreq v1 v2; simpl.
  - destruct (Nat.eqb k n) eqn:E.
    + apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. subst n.
      split; intro H.
      * destruct (Vty_value env xi U v1 v2 H) as [Hv1 Hv2].
        split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
        exact (proj2 (nth_insert_at_eq_app env k R v1 v2 Hlen) (proj2 (Hreq v1 v2) H)).
      * destruct H as [Hv1 [Hv2 H3]].
        exact (proj1 (Hreq v1 v2) (proj1 (nth_insert_at_eq_app env k R v1 v2 Hlen) H3)).
    + apply Nat.eqb_neq in E.
      inversion Hlc as [d0 i Hi | d0 X0 | d0 A0 B0 HA HB | d0 T0 H0]; subst.
      assert (Hnk : n < k) by lia.
      split; intros [H1 [H2 H3]]; (split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]]).
      * exact (proj2 (nth_insert_at_lt_app env k R n v1 v2 Hlen Hnk) H3).
      * exact (proj1 (nth_insert_at_lt_app env k R n v1 v2 Hlen Hnk) H3).
  - split; intros H; exact H.
  - inversion Hlc as [d0 i Hi | d0 X0 | d0 A0 B0 HA HB | d0 T0 H0]; subst.
    split; intros [H1 [H2 H3]]; (split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]]).
    + intros w1 w2 Hw.
      apply (proj2 (IHA env xi k R Hlen HU HA Hreq w1 w2)) in Hw.
      destruct (H3 w1 w2 Hw) as [u1 [u2 [R1 [R2 HV]]]].
      exists u1, u2. split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
      exact (proj1 (IHB env xi k R Hlen HU HB Hreq u1 u2) HV).
    + intros w1 w2 Hw.
      apply (proj1 (IHA env xi k R Hlen HU HA Hreq w1 w2)) in Hw.
      destruct (H3 w1 w2 Hw) as [u1 [u2 [R1 [R2 HV]]]].
      exists u1, u2. split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
      exact (proj2 (IHB env xi k R Hlen HU HB Hreq u1 u2) HV).
  - inversion Hlc as [d0 i Hi | d0 X0 | d0 A0 B0 HA HB | d0 T0 H0]; subst.
    split; intros [H1 [H2 H3]]; (split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]]).
    + intros rho U1 U2 HU1 HU2.
      destruct (H3 rho U1 U2 HU1 HU2) as [u1 [u2 [R1 [R2 HV]]]].
      exists u1, u2. split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
      assert (Hreq' : forall a b, R a b <-> Vty (rho :: env) xi U a b).
      { intros a b. split; intro Hx.
        - apply (proj1 (Vty_env_agree 0 U env (rho :: env) xi HU ltac:(intros i Hi0; lia) a b)).
          apply (proj1 (Hreq a b) Hx).
        - apply (proj2 (Hreq a b)).
          apply (proj2 (Vty_env_agree 0 U env (rho :: env) xi HU ltac:(intros i Hi0; lia) a b)).
          assumption. }
      exact (proj1 (IH1 (rho :: env) xi (S k) R ltac:(simpl; lia) HU H0 Hreq' u1 u2) HV).
    + intros rho U1 U2 HU1 HU2.

          assumption. }
```

```coq
Lemma Vty_open_fvar : forall env xi k T X rho,
  k <= length env ->
  ~ In X (ty_fv T) ->

    Vty (insert_at k rho env) xi T v1 v2.
```

First, helper lemmas:
```coq
Definition update_theta (theta : atom -> ty) (X : atom) (U : ty) : atom -> ty :=
  fun Y => if Nat.eqb Y X then U else theta Y.

Lemma lookup_update_eq : forall Gamma x T, lookup_context x (update Gamma x T) = Some T.
Proof. intros. unfold update. simpl. rewrite Nat.eqb_refl. reflexivity. Qed.

Lemma lookup_update_neq : forall Gamma x y T, x <> y -> lookup_context y (update Gamma x T) = lookup_context y Gamma.
Proof. intros. unfold update. simpl. destruct (Nat.eqb y x) eqn:E. apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. contradiction. reflexivity. Qed.

Lemma update_sigma_eq : forall sigma x u, update_sigma sigma x u x = u.
Proof. intros. unfold update_sigma. rewrite Nat.eqb_refl. reflexivity. Qed.

Lemma update_sigma_neq : forall sigma x y u, x <> y -> update_sigma sigma x u y = sigma y.
Proof. intros. unfold update_sigma. destruct (Nat.eqb y x) eqn:E. apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. contradiction. reflexivity. Qed.

Lemma update_theta_eq : forall theta X U, update_theta theta X U X = U.
Lemma update_theta_neq : forall theta X Y U, Y <> X -> update_theta theta X U Y = theta Y.
```


For the final theorem:
```coq
Theorem polymorphic_identity_theorem_for_free : forall t,
  has_type [] empty t
    (Ty_All (Ty_Arrow (Ty_BVar 0) (Ty_BVar 0))) ->
  forall U v,
    wf_ty [] U ->
    value v ->
    has_type [] empty v U ->
    tm_app (tm_tapp t U) v -->* v.
Proof.
  intros t Ht U v HwfU Hv HvU.
  set (rho := fun a b => a -->* v /\ b -->* v).
  pose proof (fundamental [] empty t (Ty_All (Ty_Arrow (Ty_BVar 0) (Ty_BVar 0))) Ht
              [] (fun _ => rho) (fun X => Ty_FVar X) (fun X => Ty_FVar X)
              (fun x => tm_fvar x) (fun x => tm_fvar x)) as Hf.
  (* hypotheses *)
  assert (Hl : forall X, lc_ty_at 0 ((fun X => Ty_FVar X) X)) by (intro; apply lc_ty_fvar).
  assert (Hs : forall x, lc_tm_at 0 0 ((fun x => tm_fvar x) x)) by (intro; apply lc_tm_fvar).
  specialize (Hf Hl Hl Hs Hs).
  assert (Henv : forall x Ti, lookup_context x empty = Some Ti ->
                 Rty [] (fun _ => rho) Ti ((fun x => tm_fvar x) x) ((fun x => tm_fvar x) x)).
  { intros x Ti Hlk. simpl in Hlk. discriminate. }
  specialize (Hf Henv).
  rewrite tm_ty_subst_env_id in Hf.
  rewrite tm_subst_env_id in Hf.
  ...
```
Hmm, the getm_ty_subst_env_id rewrite: Hf's conclusion is `Rty [] (fun _ => rho) T (tm_subst_env id (tm_ty_subst_env id t)) (...)`. Rewriting `tm_ty_subst_env id t` to t requires the id function to be syntactically `(fun X => Ty_FVar X)` ✓. Then tm_subst_env id t → t ✓. The rewrite order: first rewrite tm_ty_subst_env_id (the inner term), then tm_subst_env_id ✓. `rewrite tm_ty_subst_env_id in Hf` — the lemma is `forall t, tm_ty_subst_env (fun X => Ty_FVar X) t = t`; the goal contains tm_ty_subst_env (fun X => Ty_FVar X) t ✓ rewrite should fire.

Then:
```
  destruct (Rty_elim _ _ _ _ _ Hf) as [Hlc1 [Hlc2 Hex]].
  destruct Hex as [v1 [v2 [Rv1 [Rv2 HV]]]].
  destruct (Vty_all_elim [] (fun _ => rho) (Ty_Arrow (Ty_BVar 0) (Ty_BVar 0)) v1 v2 HV) as [Hvv1 [Hvv2 Hfall]].
  assert (HlcU : lc_ty_at 0 U) by (apply wf_ty_lc in HwfU; exact HwfU).
  destruct (Hfall rho U U HlcU HlcU) as [u1 [u2 [Ru1 [Ru2 HVu]]]].
  destruct (Vty_arrow_elim [rho] (fun _ => rho) (Ty_BVar 0) (Ty_BVar 0) u1 u2 HVu) as [Hvu1 [Hvu2 Hfarrow]].
  assert (Hvv : Vty [rho] (fun _ => rho) (Ty_BVar 0) v v).
  { simpl. split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
    unfold rho. split; apply multi_refl. }
  destruct (Hfarrow v v Hvv) as [s1 [s2 [Rs1 [Rs2 HVs]]]].
  destruct (Vty_bvar_elim [rho] (fun _ => rho) 0 s1 s2 HVs) as [Hvs1 [Hvs2 Hrho]].
  unfold rho in Hrho. destruct Hrho as [Hs1 Hs2].
  (* conclude: app (tapp t U) v -->* v *)
  apply multi_trans with (t2 := tm_app (tm_tapp v1 U) v).
  - apply multi_app_l.
    + apply multi_tapp. assumption. exact HlcU.
    + apply value_lc. assumption.
  - apply multi_trans with (t2 := tm_app u1 v).
    + apply multi_app_l. assumption. apply value_lc; assumption.
    + apply multi_trans with (t2 := s1).
      * assumption.
      * assumption.
Qed.
```
Wait, the first reduction: app (tapp t U) v -->* app (tapp v1 U) v. We have Rv1 : t -->* v1. multi_tapp gives tapp t U -->* tapp v1 U (needs lc U ✓). Then multi_app_l with lc v ✓ (value_lc Hv). ✓.

Hmm, careful: Hf's terms are t and t (after rewrites), and Rty gives t -->* v1 ✓.

Then app (tapp v1 U) v: we have Ru1 : tapp v1 U -->* u1 (from Vty_all_elim's existential: tapp v1 U1 -->* u1 with U1 = U ✓). multi_app_l with lc v → app (tapp v1 U) v -->* app u1 v ✓.
Then Rs1 : app u1 v -->* s1 ✓.
Then Hs1 : s1 -->* v ✓.

Great.

One concern: `Hfall rho U U HlcU HlcU` — the Vty_all_elim's clause is `forall rho U1 U2, lc U1 -> lc U2 -> exists ...`. ✓.

Also note Vty_arrow_elim [rho] ... with env = [rho] ✓ (insert_at 0 rho [] = [rho] definitional).

Also `simpl` for Hvv: Vty [rho] xi (Ty_BVar 0) v v simplifies to value v ∧ value v ∧ nth 0 [rho] bot v v = value v ∧ value v ∧ rho v v ✓. The rho is a `set` local definition; `unfold rho` gives the lambda ✓ and rho v v = v -->* v ∧ v -->* v ✓ definitional.

Actually, a cleaner approach: prove general helper lemmas for the composed substitutions:
```
Lemma lc_subst_term : forall th s t,
  (forall X, lc_ty_at 0 (th X)) -> (forall x, lc_tm_at 0 0 (s x)) ->
  lc_tm_at 0 0 t -> lc_tm_at 0 0 (tm_subst_env s (tm_ty_subst_env th t)).
Proof.
  intros. apply lc_tm_subst_env; [|assumption]. apply lc_tm_ty_subst_env; assumption.
Qed.
```
That saves repetition. Similarly for the equality computations:
```
Lemma subst_open_fvar : forall th s x t,
  (forall X, lc_ty_at 0 (th X)) -> (forall x, lc_tm_at 0 0 (s x)) ->
  tm_subst_env s (tm_ty_subst_env th (open_tm t (tm_fvar x))) =
  open_tm (tm_subst_env s (tm_ty_subst_env th t)) (s x).
Proof.
  intros th s x t Hl Hs.
  rewrite (tm_ty_subst_env_open_tm th 0 (tm_fvar x) t).
  rewrite (tm_subst_env_open_tm s 0 (tm_fvar x) (tm_ty_subst_env th t) Hs).
  simpl. reflexivity.
Qed.
```
Check: after the two rewrites, LHS = open_tm_rec 0 (tm_subst_env s (tm_fvar x)) (tm_subst_env s (tm_ty_subst_env th t)) = open_tm_rec 0 (s x) (...). And RHS = open_tm (tm_subst_env s (tm_ty_subst_env th t)) (s x) = open_tm_rec 0 (s x) (...) ✓ definitional. Need `simpl` to reduce tm_subst_env s (tm_fvar x) → s x ✓ and the open_tm definition ✓.

Hmm, `tm_ty_subst_env_open_tm th 0 (tm_fvar x) t` gives `tm_ty_subst_env th (open_tm_rec 0 (tm_fvar x) t) = open_tm_rec 0 (tm_ty_subst_env th (tm_fvar x)) (tm_ty_subst_env th t)`. The RHS has tm_ty_subst_env th (tm_fvar x) which simplifies to tm_fvar x. The rewrite turns the LHS into that RHS; then the second rewrite applies to `tm_subst_env s (open_tm_rec 0 (tm_fvar x) (tm_ty_subst_env th t))`... wait, after the first rewrite, the term is `tm_subst_env s (open_tm_rec 0 (tm_ty_subst_env th (tm_fvar x)) (tm_ty_subst_env th t))`. The second rewrite needs the form open_tm_rec 0 v t' with v = tm_ty_subst_env th (tm_fvar x), which is convertible to tm_fvar x but not syntactically. The rewrite might fail. Let me first simplify tm_ty_subst_env th (tm_fvar x) to tm_fvar x with `simpl` before the second rewrite. Let me structure:
```
Proof.
  intros th s x t Hl Hs.
  rewrite (tm_ty_subst_env_open_tm th 0 (tm_fvar x) t).
  simpl.
  rewrite (tm_subst_env_open_tm s 0 (tm_fvar x) (tm_ty_subst_env th t) Hs).
  simpl. reflexivity.
Qed.
```
After the first rewrite and simpl: LHS = tm_subst_env s (open_tm_rec 0 (tm_fvar x) (tm_ty_subst_env th t)) ✓; RHS = open_tm_rec 0 (s x) (tm_subst_env s (tm_ty_subst_env th t))? Hmm, the RHS of the equation was `open_tm (tm_subst_env s (tm_ty_subst_env th t)) (s x)` which is `open_tm_rec 0 (s x) (...)` ✓. Then the second rewrite on the LHS gives open_tm_rec 0 (tm_subst_env s (tm_fvar x)) (tm_subst_env s (tm_ty_subst_env th t)) then simpl → s x ✓.

Great, this helper will make the T_Abs case much easier.

Similarly for T_TAbs:

```
Lemma subst_open_ty_fvar : forall th s X U t,
  (forall X, lc_ty_at 0 (th X)) -> (forall x, lc_tm_at 0 0 (s x)) ->

Proof.

  ...
```
Hmm, need Hl' : forall Y, lc_ty_at 0 (update_theta th X U Y) — provable from Hl and U lc. Let me include U lc as a hypothesis:
```
Lemma subst_open_ty_fvar : forall th s X U t,
  (forall Y, lc_ty_at 0 (th Y)) -> lc_ty_at 0 U ->
  (forall x, lc_tm_at 0 0 (s x)) ->
  ~ In X (tm_ty_fv t) ->
  tm_subst_env s (tm_ty_subst_env (update_theta th X U) (open_tm_ty t (tm_fvar X))) =
  open_tm_ty (tm_subst_env s (tm_ty_subst_env th t)) U.
```
Proof:
- Let th' := update_theta th X U.
- Hl' : forall Y, lc_ty_at 0 (th' Y): destruct eqb Y X; [subst; rewrite update_theta_eq; exact HU | apply Hl].
- rewrite tm_ty_subst_env_open_tm_ty with th' Hl' d=0 U0=(tm_fvar X) t:
  tm_ty_subst_env th' (open_tm_ty_rec 0 (FVar X) t) = open_tm_ty_rec 0 (ty_subst_env th' (FVar X)) (tm_ty_subst_env th' t).
- cbn [ty_subst_env]: ty_subst_env th' (FVar X) reduces to th' X = if eqb X X then U else th X → rewrite update_theta_eq → U.
- Then tm_subst_env s of open_tm_ty_rec 0 U (...) → rewrite tm_subst_env_open_tm_ty with s Hs → open_tm_ty_rec 0 U (tm_subst_env s (tm_ty_subst_env th' t)).
- Need tm_ty_subst_env th' t = tm_ty_subst_env th t: tm_ty_subst_env_ext, for Y ∈ tm_ty_fv t, Y ≠ X (from Hfresh), so th' Y = th Y ✓ (update_theta_neq).
- Done.

Great, these helpers will make the fundamental lemma much shorter.

For the term substitution environment update: s1' = update_sigma s1 x w1. We need:
- lc images: helper `update_sigma_lc : (forall y, lc (s y)) -> lc w -> forall y, lc (update_sigma s x w y)`.
- the env condition needs: for y ≠ x, update_sigma s1 x w1 y = s1 y (update_sigma_neq).
- s1' x = w1 (update_sigma_eq).

Let me also define a helper for the env condition:
```
Lemma update_sigma_lc : forall s x w, (forall y, lc_tm_at 0 0 (s y)) -> lc_tm_at 0 0 w ->
  forall y, lc_tm_at 0 0 (update_sigma s x w y).
Proof.
  intros. unfold update_sigma. destruct (Nat.eqb y x) eqn:E.
  - apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. subst y. assumption.
  - apply H.
Qed.
```

```
Lemma lc_subst_both : forall th s t,
```

**T_TApp**:
```
  - (* T_TApp *)
    pose proof (IH1 env xi th1 th2 s1 s2 Hl1 Hl2 Hs1 Hs2 Henv) as HR1.
    pose proof (has_type_wf_lc Delta Gamma t0 (Ty_All T0) H1) as Hwl.
```

```
Lemma subst_open_fvar_update : forall th s x t w,
  (forall X, lc_ty_at 0 (th X)) -> (forall y, lc_tm_at 0 0 (s y)) ->
  lc_tm_at 0 0 w -> ~ In x (tm_fv t) ->
  tm_subst_env (update_sigma s x w) (tm_ty_subst_env th (open_tm t (tm_fvar x))) =
  open_tm (tm_subst_env s (tm_ty_subst_env th t)) w.
Proof.
  intros th s x t w Hl Hs Hw Hfresh.
  rewrite (tm_ty_subst_env_open_tm th 0 (tm_fvar x) t).
  simpl.
  rewrite (tm_subst_env_open_tm (update_sigma s x w) 0 (tm_fvar x) (tm_ty_subst_env th t) Hl').
  ...
```
where Hl' : forall y, lc (update_sigma s x w y) from Hs, Hw.
Then: open_tm_rec 0 (update_sigma s x w x) (tm_subst_env (update_sigma s x w) (tm_ty_subst_env th t)) → rewrite update_sigma_eq → w.
Then tm_subst_env (update_sigma s x w) (tm_ty_subst_env th t) = tm_subst_env s (tm_ty_subst_env th t): tm_subst_env_ext with agreement on tm_fv (tm_ty_subst_env th t) = tm_fv t; for y ∈ tm_fv t, y ≠ x (from Hfresh) → update_sigma_neq ✓.
Done.

```
Lemma ctx_fv_lookup : forall Gamma y T,
  lookup_context y Gamma = Some T -> forall X, In X (ty_fv T) -> In X (ctx_fv Gamma).

      apply in_or_app. auto.
    + apply in_or_app. right. apply (IH Hlook X HX).
Qed.
```

```coq
Theorem polymorphic_identity_theorem_for_free : forall t,
  has_type [] empty t
    (Ty_All (Ty_Arrow (Ty_BVar 0) (Ty_BVar 0))) ->
  forall U v,
    wf_ty [] U ->
    value v ->
    has_type [] empty v U ->
    tm_app (tm_tapp t U) v -->* v.
Proof.
  intros t Ht U v HwfU Hv HvU.
  set (rho := fun a b => a -->* v /\ b -->* v).
  pose proof (fundamental [] empty t (Ty_All (Ty_Arrow (Ty_BVar 0) (Ty_BVar 0))) Ht
              [] (fun _ => rho) (fun X => Ty_FVar X) (fun X => Ty_FVar X)
              (fun x => tm_fvar x) (fun x => tm_fvar x)) as Hf.
  assert (HlcTheta : forall X, lc_ty_at 0 ((fun X => Ty_FVar X) X)) by (intro; apply lc_ty_fvar).
  assert (HlcSigma : forall x, lc_tm_at 0 0 ((fun x => tm_fvar x) x)) by (intro; apply lc_tm_fvar).
  assert (Henv : forall x Ti, lookup_context x empty = Some Ti ->
                 Rty [] (fun _ => rho) Ti ((fun x => tm_fvar x) x) ((fun x => tm_fvar x) x)).
  { intros x Ti Hlk. simpl in Hlk. discriminate. }
  specialize (Hf HlcTheta HlcTheta HlcSigma HlcSigma Henv).
  rewrite tm_ty_subst_env_id in Hf.
  rewrite tm_subst_env_id in Hf.
  destruct (Rty_elim [] (fun _ => rho) (Ty_All (Ty_Arrow (Ty_BVar 0) (Ty_BVar 0))) t t Hf)
    as [HlcT1 [HlcT2 Hex]].
  destruct Hex as [w1 [w2 [Rw1 [Rw2 HV]]]].
  destruct (Vty_all_elim [] (fun _ => rho) (Ty_Arrow (Ty_BVar 0) (Ty_BVar 0)) w1 w2 HV)
    as [Hvw1 [Hvw2 Hfall]].
  assert (HlcU : lc_ty_at 0 U) by (apply (wf_ty_lc [] U); assumption).
  destruct (Hfall rho U U HlcU HlcU) as [u1 [u2 [Ru1 [Ru2 HVu]]]].
  destruct (Vty_arrow_elim [rho] (fun _ => rho) (Ty_BVar 0) (Ty_BVar 0) u1 u2 HVu)
    as [Hvu1 [Hvu2 Hfar]].
  assert (Hvv : Vty [rho] (fun _ => rho) (Ty_BVar 0) v v).
  { simpl. split; [assumption | split; [assumption |]].
    unfold rho. split; apply multi_refl. }
  destruct (Hfar v v Hvv) as [s1 [s2 [Rs1 [Rs2 HVs]]]].
  destruct (Vty_bvar_elim [rho] (fun _ => rho) 0 s1 s2 HVs) as [Hvs1 [Hvs2 Hrho]].

  - apply multi_trans with (t2 := tm_app u1 v).

      * assumption.
Qed.
```
